### 89. assistant

**Muneem is a high-ambition, local-first, privacy-critical personal-finance ledger that ingests email attachments (primarily Indian bank/CC/broker statements + promo receipts), unlocks password-protected PDFs via a hybrid strategy, parses via a verification-first "concept core" architecture, and stores results in an encrypted SQLite ledger (SQLCipher).** The promo "forgotten deals" feature is the completed Track A spine; bank statement parsing (HDFC/Axis/AU via the new engine) + hybrid unlock (C.1) are the current focus. The whole-finance vision (holdings, items, merchants, equity/MF/ETF/bond instrument families) is explicitly post-Milestone D.

I read the repo systematically per the CLAUDE.md mandate (DESIGN.md, prior-art-email-mining.md, testdata/promo-emails/README.md + all other docs first; then every src/ file, tests/, tools/, dotfiles/hooks/CI, pyproject, Makefile, .gitignore, expected.yaml, etc.). This included:

- All docs (DESIGN, ROADMAP, CURRENT_STATE, TESTING, DEPENDENCIES, parser-architecture, ingesting-statements, onboarding-a-bank, encryption-at-rest).
- Full src tree (cli, config, db/db_key, extract, offers, ocr, secrets, password_rules, unlock/statement_unlock, all parsers/* including the sophisticated engine/concepts/validation/clustering/savings/profiles + issuer modules, full ingestion/ with base + gmail/imap/outlook).
- All tests (hygiene, invariants, crypto, promo regression, parser unit + local_*, engine/validation/savings/clustering, cli, extract, db, unlock, synth, dependency licenses, registry, etc.) + synthpdf.py.
- Hooks (.githooks/pre-commit + precommit_check.py + pre-push), .github/workflows/ci.yml, tools/ auth scripts.
- Config/build (pyproject.toml with ruff/pytest/uv-friendly, Makefile).
- Corpus + answer key.

No application code was "missing" — this is a real, working early implementation with a production-grade security spine and an elegant parser architecture. Git status was clean at start; no obvious committed secrets or real statements.

### Current State vs. DESIGN + ROADMAP (as of ~2026-06 docs)
**Milestone 0 + Track A (promo spine: ingest → offers list on committed non-sensitive corpus, green regression, deterministic + OCR tiers)**: ✅ Done. Strong answer-key discipline (`testdata/promo-emails/expected.yaml`), tiered tests (T1/T3 in default CI, T2 behind `@pytest.mark.ocr`, T4 xfail), ligature NFKC handling, dedup heritage from Email-Mining (but using permitted libs), idempotent sha256 dedup.

**Track B (Gmail ingestion)**: Partial. Full OAuth2 + read-only + server-side search + attachment download in `ingestion/gmail.py` (and tools/gmail_auth.py). Outlook and IMAP are skeletal (basic search/auth only; `get_headers`/`list_pdf_attachments`/`download_attachment` raise `NotImplementedError`). No top-level `muneem fetch` CLI (ROADMAP B.4).

**Milestone C (HDFC vertical slice + unlock + parse → txns)**: Mostly landed but incomplete.
- Hybrid unlock (cached keychain + `password_rules.yaml` (config dir preferred) → prompt, keychain cache of winners; `--no-prompt`; in-memory only; wired into `cli.ingest --doc-type bank_statement` and `eval`): ✅ Excellent (C.1).
- Parsers: Concept-core (steps 1-3) landed. Verifier (per-row walk + total reconcile + bookend/SUMMARY cross-check via `validation.py`), generic L0/L1/L2 engine + `FamilySpec` (bank-agnostic, profiles-as-data in `profiles.py`), shared `savings.py` path (per-table extract_tables() candidates + coordinate clustering fallback via `clustering.py` → engine → oracle-gated). HDFC refactored as reference (keeps bespoke clustering because semi-ruled table defeats extract_tables(); 6/6 real statements reconcile + bookend). Axis (savings via engine + balance-delta reconstruction for messy columns; 6/7 real) + AU (4/4, cleanest) on shared path. CC parser exists (count-only, no running balance).
- Known gap (explicit in ROADMAP/CURRENT_STATE): Axis May 2025 still drops a line in clustering → incomplete ledger → oracle correctly quarantines (stores *nothing*, status=unreconciled). June recovered via delta. Credit-card unverified on real data.
- Schema: Per-bank tables (`hdfc_transactions`, `axis_*`, `au_transactions`) + documents/offers. No unified `transactions` + `holdings` yet (C.3 / step 5). `documents.status` carries reconciled/unreconciled. Migrations are IF NOT EXISTS + user_version (no dedicated migration scripts yet).
- `muneem txns list <bank>`, `eval <folder>` (reconcile-rate harness for onboarding), `db status/encrypt`: Present.

**Milestone D + generalize**: Early. D.2 (SQLCipher encryption-at-rest) implemented. D.1 (redacted structured logging) + D.3 (evaluation writeup) pending. Synthetic full-pipeline regression CI (ROADMAP "Planned" section: stdlib synthpdf + Layer 1 cloud + self-hosted Layer 2 for real statements) decided but **not built** (testdata/synthetic/ does not exist). L3 local-vision (DeepSeek-OCR-3B etc., reconcile-gated) research done, build deferred. Merchant canonicalization, items, holdings, analytics, instrument families: future.

**Security posture (DESIGN §5)**: One of the strongest aspects. Local-only by construction, promo corpus explicitly non-sensitive playground, keychain for all secrets (OAuth + cached passwords + 256-bit DB raw key), SQLCipher transparent AES-256 (raw-key form, no silent downgrade), OS perms hardening, no decrypted PDF written to disk, reconciliation *never stores unreconciled rows*, aggressive git hygiene (pre-commit hook + test + .gitignore), license allowlist enforced in CI, no PyMuPDF. Threat model stated honestly (keychain + SQLCipher does not defend malware-as-logged-in-user; passphrase mode is deferred opt-in).

**Testing & process**: Excellent foundation. Offline/secretless default CI (matrix 3.11-3.13), markers for ocr/llm/local_statements, autouse keychain isolation, DB invariant tripwires + schema lock (update lock in same PR = explicit allow), pre-push local gate (lint + full offline suite), pre-commit sensitive-file blocker, dependency license test, synth word-list + some full-pipeline tests, grounded corpus inspection in TESTING.md. Real statements never in CI/repo.

**Gaps visible even in early state**: No logging framework at all in src/. Broad `except Exception` + bare RuntimeError in several parser paths. Partial ingestion impls. No mypy/pyright. Unified schema + holdings not started. Real-data precision not yet 100% on Axis. No `muneem fetch`, limited CLI surfaces, no backup/restore/WAL commands (docs note the risk), synthetic regression harness deferred, no Tier-2 semantics or merchant handling.

The project shows real discipline (attribution, "never invent," explicit parked decisions, "extraction proposes, reconciliation disposes," commercial-license rigor). It is already far ahead of a typical greenfield personal tool on the security/hygiene axis.

### Bulleted List of Code-Level and Architectural Improvements
For a **commercial-grade** bar (reliability under real Indian statement drift, auditability, zero-leak invariants, extensibility to whole-finance without rewrites, ops/debuggability for the single user over years, maintainability by one person or small trusted set, defensibility on security review), the following are needed. Prioritized roughly by impact/risk.

**Architectural / Cross-Cutting (high bar — do not silently lock in open decisions):**
- **Implement structured, redacted logging (D.1) immediately.** DESIGN/ROADMAP call for it; currently zero logging in src/. Must have levels, redaction of any account numbers/balances/narrations/PII/passwords (even in errors), structured (JSON or key-value) output, and tests that feed synthetic sensitive values and assert masking. Never `print()` or bare `str(exc)` containing statement text. This is table stakes for any ledger that touches full financial life.
- **Complete the regression CI plan (ROADMAP "Planned" section).** Build the two-layer system: (1) synthetic full-pipeline PDFs via `synthpdf.py` (already exists; expand for HDFC/Axis/AU layouts + bookends) exercised on every push in cloud CI; (2) self-hosted runner layer that stages real (gitignored) statements from a runner-local path + `password_rules.yaml` (no GitHub secrets for passwords). Trigger on all-branch pushes. This is the only way to catch parser drift on real vintages without ever putting statements in git history.
- **Unify storage + introduce instrument family (step 5 / C.3 / whole-finance scope).** Per-bank tables are a temporary fidelity crutch. Move to canonical `transactions` (with provenance + confidence columns) + `holdings` + `accounts` + `merchants`/`items`. Make the engine truly family-agnostic for equity/MF/ETF/bond events (buy/sell/dividend/fee) with new `FamilySpec` + oracle. Keep per-bank as views or migration artifacts.
- **L3 vision fallback + golden eval harness.** Wire the researched DeepSeek-OCR-3B (4-bit + SDPA on 2070S-class) as rare fallback only on reconcile failure (self-consistency resampling), behind the same oracle. Build the local-golden + synthetic-golden + (approval-gated cloud-golden) sets per parser-architecture.md §10a. `muneem eval` is the seed; extend it.
- **Resolve or explicitly time-box parked decisions.** LLM locality for *sensitive* docs (DESIGN §8.3) and merchant canonicalization (§8.5) must surface with user input before generalization; do not implement cloud paths for bank data even experimentally without per-session approval + redaction + test gates. Document the hardware/latency constraints.
- **Add proper schema migrations + a docs/schema.md.** Current IF NOT EXISTS + pragma is fine for early; for commercial you need versioned, reversible, tested migrations (Alembic-lite or hand-rolled with checksums) and a living schema document. The invariant lock is great — keep it.
- **Strengthen ingestion completeness before heavy parser work.** Finish Outlook/IMAP attachment paths or clearly mark as experimental/deferred. Build the `muneem fetch` CLI (incremental via historyId/deltaLink, sender allowlist + heuristics for "financial document", staging dir cataloging into documents rows with locked/unparsed status). Make detection and dedup robust.
- **Add Tier-2 transaction semantics + merchant canonicalization foundation.** Once unified schema lands, add counterparty/merchant extraction + fuzzy + override table (the "mess" will appear on real receipts). Same for itemized receipts (Swiggy etc.). This is core to the §2 use-cases.
- **Observability / ops surface for a long-lived single-user tool.** `muneem db backup` (proper WAL checkpoint + Online Backup API, not naive cp), key rotation scaffolding (the deferred passphrase path), ledger health/verify, export (CSV/JSON for the user's own analysis), query filters on txns list beyond limit, basic analytics surfaces. Document backup/restore and "what if keychain is lost" procedures.
- **Packaging, distribution, and reproducibility for commercial use.** uv + editable is fine for dev. For real use: pinned lockfile (or requirements.txt equivalent), SBOM generation in CI, a simple installer story (or "just pip install -e in a venv" with clear instructions), Windows/Linux/macOS path/secret-backend testing matrix. Consider a small local web UI later but keep CLI authoritative.

**Code-Level / Implementation (reliability, correctness, maintainability, security hardening):**
- **Error handling & exception hygiene (critical).** Replace broad `except Exception as exc: raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to parse {pdf_path}: {exc}")` (seen in axis/au/hdfc paths) with a small hierarchy (`MuneemError`, `ParseError` (with non-sensitive context only), `UnlockError`, `EncryptedDatabaseError` already good). Never put raw statement text, passwords, or full paths with PII into exception messages that might be logged. Add specific handling for pdfplumber/pypdf/pdfminer failures, corrupt PDFs, unexpected layouts. Every external call (pdf libs, keyring, yaml, sql) must be wrapped with attribution of what failed.
- **Introduce a logging + redaction module now.** Even before full structured logs, centralize all output that could contain sensitive data. Add provenance (which layer/mapping/profile produced a parse) to every row and document it.
- **Type safety.** Add mypy (or pyright) to dev deps + CI (strict). Many places use loose `object`, bare `dict`, `sqlite3.Row` without protocols. `Cell = object`, family build callables, etc. benefit from tighter contracts. Ruff is good but insufficient alone for commercial Python.
- **Parser robustness & the known Axis gap.** Invest in the clusterer (better line recovery, coverage for the May 2025 drop case, more synthetic matrices that reproduce real vintage drift). Make coordinate clustering and engine output carry richer diagnostics (without values) for `eval`. Add property-based tests (hypothesis) over synthetic matrices for the verifier/engine. Increase synthetic PDF coverage in `synthpdf.py` to hit more header variants, wrapped narrations, multi-account sections, SUMMARY drift.
- **PDF library surface area.** You have pdfminer.six (text), pypdf (decrypt), pdfplumber (tables+words), pypdfium2 (render). Audit overlap and pin exact versions. Ensure password is *always* passed through and never persisted. Consider whether a thinner extraction abstraction would reduce surface.
- **Secrets / password / key handling hardening.** `password_rules.yaml` loader has a deprecation path — good. Add validation (no empty passwords, quote-numeric reminder). Ensure `_remember` and keychain JSON lists are size-capped or aged. Add tests that assert *no* password material appears in any log, exception, or stdout even on failure paths (already partially done; make exhaustive). Test the full hybrid order + caching + `--no-prompt` + legacy CWD warning.
- **DB / crypto invariants (already strong — make them stronger).** The `test_db_invariants.py` + crypto tests + schema lock are excellent. Add: WAL checkpoint + backup API tests, rekey/rotation path tests (even if deferred), performance guard on large ledgers, explicit test that `connect_encrypted` on a plaintext file *always* raises the helpful message. Harden the `encrypt_database` temp-file cleanup and verification.
- **CLI & UX for trust.** `txns list` output is minimal/raw. Add columns for status/provenance, filters (date range, amount, account), and a "verify" subcommand that re-runs reconciliation on stored rows. `ingest` and `eval` must never echo statement content. Add machine-readable output (JSON) behind a flag.
- **Testing gaps to close for commercial bar.** 
  - Full end-to-end with mocked providers (no real OAuth).
  - More negative/adversarial fixtures (bad dates, transposed digits, ligatures, zero-amount rows, multi-currency, footer/header pollution).
  - Snapshot/golden for unified extracted text per issuer vintage (non-sensitive synthetic only).
  - Fuzzing or mutation testing on the verifier (a single off-by-one in reconcile must be caught).
  - Explicit tests for every security assertion in TESTING.md §9 (some exist; make the log-redaction and cloud-LLM-category-gate assertions ironclad).
  - Headless Linux keyring fallback tests.
- **Hygiene & process.** Add mypy + (optional) bandit/safety to lint. Consider a small CONTRIBUTING or "how to add a bank" that points to onboarding-a-bank.md + eval. Pin the exact synthpdf layout expectations so clusterer changes are deliberate. Expand the pre-push gate to include the license and hygiene tests explicitly if not already covered by `pytest`.
- **Smaller code smells.** Remove dead back-compat aliases where possible (or clearly mark). Centralize date/money regexes more. Make `safe_amount` vs `parse_amount` distinction (0.00 handling) documented in one place. Add `__version__` from git or build metadata instead of hardcoded 0.0.1. Consistent use of `pathlib` vs str. Consider a small `models` or `domain` layer if per-bank tables grow.
- **Performance / scale (personal but real statements can be 30-100 pages).** Profile large PDFs. Image dedup (MD5 + SSIM heritage) should be wired for statement chrome once L3 or heavy OCR lands. Memory-map or streaming where pdfplumber holds everything.
- **Docs & attribution.** Produce the promised `docs/parser-notes-hdfc.md` (and per-issuer) as part of D.3. Keep "memory" references (hdfc-parser-tech-decision etc.) either in-repo or removed. Maintain the decision log in ROADMAP §1.

**Overall assessment**: The foundation (security invariants, reconciliation-as-oracle philosophy, profiles-as-data, hygiene automation, non-sensitive regression corpus with ground truth) is already at or above commercial bar for a personal tool. The parser architecture is thoughtful and the right shape for the "formats change yearly" reality of Indian banks. The main risks to commercial viability are (1) silent or brittle parsing on real drift (mitigated by oracle but needs the regression CI and clustering recovery), (2) absence of operational visibility (logging, backup, health), and (3) incomplete vertical slice (fetch + unified schema + 100% real-data precision on the first banks).

Implement the deferred regression CI + logging + error hygiene + schema unification next; they unlock safe generalization. Keep the "ask before new services / cloud for sensitive / weaken gitignore" discipline — it is the project's superpower.

The project is on a strong trajectory. These improvements will make it defensible for long-term personal (or even small trusted-circle) use with full financial data. Let me know which area to dive deeper on or start implementing (with a plan).
